## `AGO-3220-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01`

- **Country:** `AGO` · **Track:** `ENG`
- **Role:** Acoustic-Impedance Metrologist
- **Scenario:** Steady-State Process Parameter Micro-Tuning & Drift Compensation Calibration SOP for Musical Instrument Acoustic Resonance & Frequency Spectrum
- **Intro:** Acoustic-Impedance Metrologist — Steady-State Process Parameter Micro-Tuning & Drift Compensation Calibration SOP for Musical Instrument Acoustic Resonance & Frequency Spectrum — Environmental Stabilization and Pre-Calibration Check: Temperature: 23.0°C ± 0.5°C; RH: 45% ± 5%; Ambient noise: ≤ 35 dB(A); Warm-up time: ≥ 30 min; Acclimation time: ≥ 2 hours.
- **Key tolerance:** Temperature: 23.0°C ± 0.5°C; RH: 45% ± 5%; Ambient noise: ≤ 35 dB(A); Warm-up time: ≥ 30 min; Acclimation time: ≥ 2 hours.
